HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001","root_001272/B007","root_001272/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_019a5e060e7cedfc91e6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\",\"B007\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Söyleme buyruğu, inancı içte tutulan bir düşünce olmaktan çıkarıp başkalarına aktarılabilen açık bir bildirime dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0468a3f28892251b0453","text":"5. **Unshared status, not disconnected solitude.** The fixed root's `ء ح د:B005` can activate isolation or being alone, and 112:3-4 initially sharpens that possibility by removing offspring and counterpart. Yet 112:0's `ر ح م:B001` establishes beneficent relation and 112:2's `ص م د:B001` establishes the approach of dependents. Those relations correct the isolation branch: I infer that relation can remain real without distributing the subject's status among peers. `أَحَدٌ` thus reads as unshared identity and rank, not as absence of care, address, or relational traffic.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2adb19a77070b7bdeb39","text":"3. **The first `أحد` is sharpened by the last `أحد`: absolute one becomes no-anyone.** Lexical evidence: `ء ح د B001` in 112:1 (oneness and absolute one), `ء ح د B002` in 112:4 (negative exhaustive anyone), and `ك ف ء B001` (equal counterpart). Structural cue: the same surface-root appears at the opening assertion and closing negation; the first is indefinite affirmative predicate, the second indefinite under negation. Assigned roles: first `أحد` states uniqueness; final `أحد` scans the field of possible persons and exhausts it under \"no counterpart.\" Abductive move: I infer that the closing negated use retroactively prevents the opening `أحد` from being read as merely numerical \"one among countables.\" Reading change: 112:1 reads as absolute unshared singularity that has already excluded the possibility of a comparable \"anyone.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2edfa31708f197da3577","text":"3. **The named subject as the destination of devotion and need.** Fixed-ayah `ٱللَّهُ` supports `ء ل ه:B001` (the worshipped one) as well as `ء ل ه:B002` (the fixed divine name). Its recurrence from 112:0 through 112:2 culminates in apposition with `ٱلصَّمَدُ`, whose `ص م د:B001` is the one intentionally resorted to and relied upon. The name identifies the subject, worship assigns the human orientation toward Him, and resort supplies the practical movement of needs in that same direction. I infer that devotion and dependence form one directional relation because the text juxtaposes their roots without explicitly stating the causal link. The clause thus reads `ٱللَّهُ` not as a bare proper name but as the uniquely named destination of worshipful reliance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B016"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d5c03f03569c2b5261d","text":"2. **A commanded definition rather than a passing report.** The same fixed root offers `ق و ل:B016` (a definition or essence stated as qawl). After `قُلْ`, the packet presents two compact positive predicates in 112:1-2 and then paired exclusions in 112:3-4; `ٱللَّهُ` is also repeated as the subject at the opening of the first two positive statements. This sequence assigns the saying the role of setting a boundary around what the fixed designation can and cannot denote. The abductive move is that the ordered predicates and exclusions function together as a definition, although the packet never labels them as such. `قُلْ` consequently introduces a concise act of defining, not only the reporting of an isolated fact.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B002","root_001272/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_40ed5644cebd55b30f59","text":"2. **The speech corrects public attribution and circulating talk.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B005` (false saying or false attribution), `ق و ل B007` (circulating talk/public report), `و ل د B005` (generated/derived thing), `ك ف ء B001` (equal counterpart), and `ء ح د B002` in the final negated `أحد`. Structural cue: the positive command and declaration in 112:1 are followed by two negated clauses: no begetting/being begotten, no counterpart as anyone. Assigned roles: `قل` becomes the authorized counter-speech; the negated birth and counterpart clauses identify what kinds of wrong public saying are being fenced off. Abductive move: I supply the social-polemical arrow that an imperative to say a concise formula responds to possible claims in circulation; the packet supplies `ق و ل` and the subsequent denials. Reading change: 112:1 is not isolated affirmation; it is a corrective utterance that pre-empts derivative, filial, or equivalence attributions.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_50da5394d59824ee69ad","text":"112:4 most changed this ayah: the final `أحد` under negation made the first `أحد` feel less numerical and more like an assertion tested against an exhausted field of possible counterparts. 112:2 also surprised the reading of `قل`: because `الصمد` is so dense and predicate-like, the command to say became a command to state an essence-boundary. 112:3 corrected the social frame of the utterance by showing that the false attributions to be countered are not vague polytheistic plurality only, but specifically generation, derivation, parenthood, and peer-producing origin.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_62548cc935d2eee7d0a3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:4\",\"root_000017\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Buradaki birlik, ilişkisiz bir yalnızlık değildir; merhamet ve kendisine yönelme ilişkileri sürerken Allah'ın kimliği ve konumu paylaşılmaz kalır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000047/B001","root_000047/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_7612a2fa6b645bd8a568","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:3\",\"root_000047\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Allah adı, sonraki dayanak niteliğiyle birlikte, ibadetin ve ihtiyacın aynı yönde toplandığı özneyi gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B002","root_000017/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7bad635174f7348e71db","text":"112:4's second `أَحَدٌ` was the decisive retrospective activation: its negative `ء ح د:B002` converted the first occurrence from possible numerical singularity into absolute oneness tested against every candidate for `ك ف ء:B001` equivalence. Meanwhile, 112:2's resort relation prevented dormant fixed `ء ح د:B005` from hardening into disconnected isolation, and the ordered predicates and exclusions of 112:2-4 made fixed `ق و ل:B016` visible as definition only after the full sequence had been read.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B005","root_001272/B007","root_001272/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9a95948d7984d5106e9b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B005\",\"B007\",\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu söyleyiş, dolaşımdaki soy, türeme ve denklik yakıştırmalarını önceden karşılayan düzeltici bir söz işlevi görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9f4f43921474875c44ec","text":"4. **Oneness resists both rank-rivalry and peerhood.** Lexical evidence: `ء ح د B001/B005` (oneness; isolation), `س م و B007` (rivaling by vying upward) from the opening root inventory, `و ل د B006` (peer of the same birth-age), and `ك ف ء B001` (equal peer/counterpart). Structural cue: an initial raised name is followed by `أحد`, while later birth-based peerhood and counterpart equality are denied. Assigned roles: `أحد` marks non-participation in a competitive scale; `كفوًا` and `لدة`-like peerhood are the possible social/material equivalents refused by the surrounding ayat. Abductive move: I infer that if something is called one in a context saturated by name, birth, and equivalence, the local mechanism is not arithmetic but removal from rival classes. Reading change: `أحد` becomes solitude from rank competition, matching, and same-origin peerhood.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B002","root_000017/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a2d5163845d665515769","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:4\",\"root_000017\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İlk ayetteki birlik, son ayette mümkün her dengi dışlayan kapsamlı olumsuzlamayla sınanır; böylece yalnızca sayısal bir birlik olmaktan çıkar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a79d7da76f85cbe10a5a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:4\",\"root_000017\",[\"B001\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Birlik, ortak kökenli akranların veya yükselme yarışındaki rakiplerin oluşturduğu bir sınıfa girmemeyi de bildirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001","root_001272/B016"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_acaaee6005f6e238b60e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:1:1\",\"root_001272\",[\"B001\",\"B016\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Buyruktan sonra gelen kısa olumlu nitelemeler ve onları izleyen olumsuzlamalar, sözü geçici bir haberden çok sınırları çizilmiş bir tanıma dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d39849f94db70be60c6d","text":"1. **Speech made outward and repeatable.** Fixed-ayah `قُلْ` is directly anchored in `ق و ل:B001` (uttered speech), with latent `ق و ل:B007` supplying circulating public report in contrast to `ق و ل:B012`'s unspoken inner saying. The imperative form, followed by a compact declaration, structurally requires the thought to cross from inward conception into articulation; 112:0's `س م و:B005` designation gives that articulation a named referent. I infer from the imperative, not from an explicit audience marker, that the utterance is meant to be transmitted beyond the commanded speaker. The ayah therefore reads as the launching of a repeatable proclamation, not merely permission to hold a private belief.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B002","root_000017/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ea259b740eab91dd579e","text":"4. **Positive oneness completed by exhaustive exclusion.** Fixed-ayah `أَحَدٌ` directly activates `ء ح د:B001` (oneness and absolute one). The same surface returns at 112:4 inside `لَمْ ... أَحَدٌ`, where `ء ح د:B002` supplies exhaustive \"anyone\" under negation, and `ك ف ء:B001` names the equality being denied. The first occurrence establishes singular unity; the final occurrence tests the entire field for a matching member and finds none. The backward link is inferred from lexical repetition and the opening/closing placement. The first `أَحَدٌ` therefore reads as qualitative, unmatched oneness rather than merely the numeral one of `ء ح د:B003`.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001272/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ee0ebc00f413a0532814","text":"1. **The command to say functions as a definition, not merely an utterance.** Lexical evidence: `ق و ل B001` (uttered speech), `ق و ل B016` (definition or essence stated as qawl), `ء ل ه B002`, `ء ح د B001` (absolute oneness), and later `ص م د B002` (compact solidity without hollowness). Structural cue: the imperative `قل` is followed by a pronoun, the repeated divine name, and a one-word predicate; 112:2 then gives another one-word predicate. Assigned roles: `قل` provides articulated form; `هو الله أحد` supplies the compressed definition; `الصمد` gives the defined One a dense, non-hollow support-image. Abductive move: I infer that the packet's extreme nominal compression makes the utterance definitional: the supplied words are not narrative events but essence-clauses. Reading change: 112:1 reads as \"voice this boundary-definition of the referent\" rather than simply \"say a sentence about God.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
